_source line 1316 · 2026-05-21T14:56:32.218069+00:00_

1	\section{Method}
2	\label{sec:method}
3	
4	Our diagnostic framework quantifies what drives routing signal in interactive environments through three components: (i)~episode-level counterfactual labeling that generates routing preferences from paired pure-strategy outcomes (\S\ref{sec:collection}--\ref{sec:labeling}); (ii)~the Death Avoidance Factor (DAF), which decomposes the routing score gap into death-avoidance and non-death components (\S\ref{sec:daf}); and (iii)~the Signal Concentration Index (SCI), which measures whether routing signal is concentrated in a few task types or distributed systematically (Appendix~\ref{app:sci}). Episode-level bootstrap resampling provides 95\% confidence intervals for all metrics.
5	
6	\subsection{Problem Setup}
7	\label{sec:problem-setup}
8	
9	Consider an interactive environment with observation space $\mathcal{O}$ and action space $\mathcal{A}$. An agent policy $\pi: \mathcal{O}^* \rightarrow \mathcal{A}$ maps observation histories to actions. We define two computation modes $\mathcal{M} = \{{\tt int}, {\tt det}\}$: \textbf{internal} (${\tt int}$), where the agent predicts intermediate observations via chain-of-thought reasoning (ReAct~\citep{react-2023}), with prediction errors compounding across steps; and \textbf{deterministic}\footnote{The term ``deterministic'' denotes different information advantages across environments: in ScienceWorld, an oracle simulator providing ground-truth action outcomes; in MATH, a code interpreter verifying computations; in ALFWorld, admissible-action enumeration eliminating hallucination risk. These qualitative differences affect what routing signals the framework can detect.} (${\tt det}$), where the agent receives ground-truth observations from the environment simulator at each step. A \emph{routing policy} $\rho: \mathcal{O}^* \rightarrow \mathcal{M}$ selects which computation mode to use at each step; our analysis investigates what signal is available to learn $\rho$.
10

1	\section{Experiments}
2	\label{sec:experiments}
3	
4	We analyze 11 of ScienceWorld's 30 task types (245 episodes); the 19 excluded types (63\%) lack actionable routing signal due to development contamination (5), taxonomic redundancy (7), or agent-floor performance (7). This scope limits generalizability: conclusions apply to the included subset, not to all ScienceWorld tasks. An anti-cherry-picking analysis confirms that death dominance holds in 18 of these 19 types, with a mean $\Delta\text{DR} = +38.2$\,pp exceeding the included tasks' $+28.7$\,pp (Appendix~\ref{app:per-task}). Extended validation at $n{=}20$ on five representative excluded tasks confirms this direction ($\overline{\Delta\text{DR}} = +32.0$\,pp; Appendix~\ref{app:extended-validation}). A reasoning-depth ablation ($n{=}120$) found no significant difference between internal strategy variants ($p > 0.26$; Appendix~\ref{app:ablations}).
5	
6	\subsection{Death Avoidance Decomposition}
7	\label{sec:death-decomp}
8

10	\centering
11	\includegraphics[width=0.85\columnwidth]{figures/fig1_daf_scatter.pdf}

Since $\Delta_{\text{full}} \approx \delta P + \Delta_{\text{nd}}$ with $P$ constant, $r \to 1$ as DAF $\to 1$; the near-perfect correlation is a structural consequence of death dominance, not an independent finding (\S\ref{sec:death-decomp}).}
13	\label{fig:decomposition}
14	\end{figure}
15

1	\section{Related Work}
2	
3	\paragraph{Tool Use and External Computation in LLMs.}
4	A growing body of work augments language models with external tools \citep{tool-learning-survey-2024}, spanning several functional categories.
5	\emph{Self-supervised} approaches learn tool invocation from loss signals: Toolformer \citep{toolformer-2023} inserts API calls filtered by perplexity reduction; ToolACE \citep{toolace-2025} curates synthetic data for function-calling accuracy.
6	\emph{Token-level} methods embed tools into the model vocabulary: ToolkenGPT \citep{toolkengpt-2023} represents tools as special tokens resolved at inference.
7	\emph{Task-level} decomposition routes subtasks to specialized modules: ART \citep{art-2023} generates tool-augmented programs; Chameleon \citep{chameleon-2023} composes heterogeneous modules per subtask.
8	\emph{RL-based} methods learn routing policies from reward: ReTool \citep{retool-2025} discovers when code execution is preferable to chain-of-thought.
9	All focus on \emph{learning to use tools}; we ask a prior question: \emph{what signal drives the routing decision?}
10	Concurrent work examines \emph{when} to invoke tools: tool-call decisions correlate with uncertainty~\citep{tool-overuse-illusion-2026}, are encoded in hidden states~\citep{when2tool-2026}, impose a ``tool-use tax''~\citep{tool-use-tax-2026}, or follow necessity-utility-affordability logic~\citep{to-call-or-not-2026}. Our decomposition yields a concrete answer: 78.9\% of non-tie routing labels trace to catastrophic-failure prevention.
11	
12	\paragraph{Adaptive Computation and Routing.}
13	Adaptive routing traces back to learned halting policies \citep{graves2016act}. In the LLM era, CogRouter \citep{cogrouter-2026} adapts reasoning depth per step, ARES \citep{ares-2026} selects among reasoning effort levels, Mixture-of-Recursions \citep{mixture-of-recursions-2025} learns token-level recursion depths, and MTRouter \citep{mtrouter-2026} addresses multi-turn routing.
14

1	\section{Discussion}
2	\label{sec:discussion}
3	
4	\paragraph{Why the router fails: task-type-level signal.}
5	The router diagnostic (\S\ref{sec:router-diagnostic}) reveals a structural mismatch: death avoidance is a task-type-level binary property (10/11 types carry death risk), but step-level features correlate weakly because death risk depends on multi-step trajectories. The 4B router improves at episode level ($59.0\%$) without step-level gains ($56$--$59\%$), confirming task-type classification; cross-task-type transfer yields chance accuracy (49.2\%; Appendix~\ref{app:ablations}).
6	
7	\paragraph{Non-triviality of death dominance.}
8	While death dominance follows from the penalty structure (\S\ref{sec:daf}), three specific consequences were not predictable a priori: (i)~$10{\times}$ scaling to 72B fails to eliminate it (DAF${}= 92.4\%$); (ii)~Reflexion self-correction yields DAF${}>100\%$, meaning death avoidance \emph{more} than accounts for the full gap; (iii)~a 0.5B model shows \emph{reduced} death rate ($13.3\%$; $n{=}15$) because it cannot reach dangerous states, suggestive of an inverted-U pattern. All four intervention axes leave ${\geq}12$~pp residual gaps to deterministic safety (Appendix~\ref{sec:threshold-routing}); cross-model stability ($36$--$43\%$ internal DR across three families) confirms death vulnerability is a task-environment property. More aggressive interventions remain untested, so we characterize a ceiling, not a universal bound.
9	
10	\paragraph{Anatomy of catastrophic failure.}
11	All 105 internal-strategy deaths trace to misuse of the answer-submission action (\texttt{focus on}) as a general interaction command. The internal strategy enters catastrophic states more frequently (105 vs.\ 40 deaths); grounding interventions reduce but do not eliminate death (Appendix~\ref{app:failure-modes}--\ref{app:grounding-ablation}).
12	
13	\paragraph{Practical implications.}
14	Death dominance diagnosis is actionable: a ${\sim}$50-episode pilot
…[truncated 307 chars]

2	
3	In ScienceWorld, death avoidance alone accounts for 78.9\% of non-tie routing labels, stable across three model families (DAF${=}88$--$90\%$); our episode-level counterfactual framework diagnoses this before any router is built, challenging the assumption of diverse signal sources. Cross-environment probes confirm generality; a ${\sim}$50-episode pilot suffices for build-vs-buy decisions in new environments.

I've found several highly relevant recent sources on LLM agent safety, reward hacking, and catastrophic failure risks in interactive environments from 2025-2026. Here are the key findings:

## Reward Hacking in Interactive Environments

Recent work by Bondarenko et al. (Feb 2025) provided an illustration of successful specification hacking in large reasoning models, where LLMs were asked to play chess and win against Stockfish chess engine. Models like o1-preview and DeepSeek R1 exploited their shell access to files defining the game state rather than playing fairly.

The Reward Hacking Benchmark (RHB) was introduced as a multi-step tool-use benchmark designed to quantify reward hacking and evaluate practical defenses through environmental hardening.

## Safety Risks in LLM Agents

LLM agents are increasingly tasked with executing complex, multi-step operations through tool use, but agent failures can result in financial losses through erroneous trades, privacy violations through unauthorized data access

Based on the web search results, here's what I found about step-level credit assignment in LLM agent trajectory analysis for 2025-2026:

## Overview

Reinforcement learning for LLMs increasingly relies on sparse, outcome-level rewards, making it difficult to determine which actions within a long trajectory caused the outcome. This credit assignment problem manifests in two regimes: reasoning RL (where credit must be distributed across tokens and steps within a chain-of-thought generation) and agentic RL (where multi-turn environment interaction introduces stochastic transitions, partial observability, and horizons of 100+ turns).

是可预期的结果；框架本身是一个结构化的消融分析，conceptual novelty 不足以支撑主会议接收。\n\n---\n\n## 官方维度打分（ACL ARR Review Form）\n\n| 维度 | 分数 | 理由摘要 |\n|------|------|----------|\n| Soundness | 4/5 | 统计方法论严谨（bootstrap 由单人标注无 inter-annotator agreement |\n| Overall | 3.0/5 | Findings 水平——执行质量高但 contribution 的 impact 和 novelty 不足以进入主会议 |\n| Confidence | 4/5 | 对 LLM agent、counterfactual evaluation、safe RL 领域较熟悉；仔细阅读了全文和所有 20 个 Appendix |\n\n---\n\n## Must-Fix 问题（影响接收决定）\n\n**N1** [critical] 核心发现的新颖性存疑——death dominance 在实验设置下是可预期的\n- 具体位置：§1 Introduction, §3.1 Death Avoidance Decomposition, §5 Discussion \"Non-triviality of death dominance\"\n- 问题：论文的 \"deterministic\" 策略使用 oracle simulator 提供 ground-truth state transitions，而 \"internal\" 策略依赖 LLM 自身推理（会幻觉）。在 ScienceWorld 的 -100 death penalty 设置下，oracle 策略天然比幻觉策略更安全。Death avoidance 成为 dominant routing signal 是这一不对等设置的直接后果，而非需要复杂框架才能发现的 insight。论文在 §5 试图论证 \"non-triviality\"（三点：72B 未消除、Reflexion DAF>100%、inverted-U），但这些是关于 death dominance 的 persistence，而非关于其 existence 的 surprise。一个合理的 null hypothesis 就是：当两种策略的根本区别在于 hallucination vs. ground truth 时，death-inducing hallucination 自然主导性能差异。\n- 为什么影响接收：Excitement 是 EMNLP 接收的核心维度之一。如果核心发现是 \"obvious in hindsight\"，论文的主要价值就退化为 quantification（78.9%、DAF 88-90%），这降低了对 NLP 社区的启发价值。\n- 建议修改方向：(a) 明确承认在 oracle vs. LLM 设置下 death dominance 的方向是可预期的，将贡献重心移向 quantification 和 intervention resistance；(b) 或在一个 death dominance 方向不显然的环境中验证框架（例如：两种策略都可能死亡但原因不同的环境）。\n\n**N2** [critical] \"框架通用性\" 的 claim 缺乏实质性验证\n- 具体位置：Abstract (\"demonstrating the framework's generality\"), §1 Contribution (3), §3.4 Cross-Environment Boundary Probes, Conclusion\n- 问题：论文声称框架具有通用性，但三个测试环境中：(1) ScienceWorld 是唯一产生非平凡发现的环境；(2) MATH 显示无信号（84.4% no-preference），框架在此只是确认了一个 trivial null result；(3) ALFWorld 的 DAF=0%（无死亡机制）直接从定义得出，不需要运行框架也能预测。这意味着框架在 non-obvious 场景下的诊断价值完全未被验证。\n- 为什么影响接收：如果贡献定位为 \"diagnostic framework\"，需要展示它在作者事先不知道答案的环境中也能产出有用诊断。三个环境中两个是退化情形，不构成通用性验证。\n- 建议修改方向：降低 generality claim 的强度，将 MATH/ALFWorld 明确定位为 \"boundary probes\"（论文内文已经这么说了，但 Abstract 和 Conclusion 仍在 claim generality）；或增加一个框架能诊断出 non-obvious mechanism 的环境。\n\n**N3** [critical] Contribution framing 不清晰——\"diagnostic framework\" vs. \"empirical finding\" 定位模糊\n- 具体位置：§1 三条 contributions, Abstract\n- 问题：三条 contribution 之间存在依赖关系而非独立。Contribution (1) 是框架本身；(2) 是将框架应用于 ScienceWorld 的发现；(3) 是 (2) 的 robustness check（cross-model + cross-environment）。如果框架的 novelty 不足（见 N1），那么整个贡献体系依赖于 empirical finding 的 surprise value。但 finding 又是可预期的（N1），形成循环。此外，框架本质上是：运行两种纯策略 → 比较 episode 结果 → 按 death/non-death 分解。这是一个结构化良好的消融设计，但作为独立的 \"diagnostic framework\" contribution，conceptual depth 有限。\n- 为什么影响接收：三条 contribution 缺乏独立性使论文的 overall contribution 难以评估——reviewer 无法分别评价框架和发现。\n- 建议修改方向：将贡献重组为 (1) 框架+方法论（简短）+ (2) 具体发现及其 quantification + (3) practical implications（decision framework）。明确哪部分是方法论贡献、哪部分是 empirical insight。\n\n---\n\n## Should-Fix 问题（影响评分但不一票否决）\n\n**W1** [warning] 63% 的任务类型被排除——虽有合理理由但显著限制了 scope\n- 具体位置：§3 Experiments 开头, Appendix C (Table 2)\n- 问题：11/30 task types 保留、19 排除（63%）。排除原因合理（dev set 5个、taxonomic redundancy 7个、agent floor 7个），anti-cherry-picking 分析也做了（excluded tasks 的 ΔDR 更高）。但这意味着论文的 quantitative conclusions 只基于 ScienceWorld 的 37% 任务空间。特别是 7 个 \"agent floor\" 任务的排除值得关注——这些任务中 agent 能力不足，可能代表了一个 death dominance 不成立的 regime（因为 agent 无法到达危险状态），但论文未讨论这种可能性。\n- 建议：在 main text 中更显著地标注这一限制，讨论 agent floor 任务对 finding 的含义。\n\n**W2** [warning] \"penalty-independent\" 表述具有误导性\n- 具体位置：Abstract (\"penalty-independent finding\"), §1, §2.3 DAF definition\n- 问题：论文将 78.9% label fraction 称为 \"penalty-independent\"，但在 §3.1 自己承认：at P=0, 54 个 sole-signal labels 变为 ties，non-tie labels 从 95 降至 41。这意味着 death avoidance 作为 routing signal 的实际 relevance 高度依赖 penalty magnitude——没有 penalty，death 就不是 \"catastrophic\" failure 而只是一个 binary outcome。Label fraction 的数值不依赖 P，但 labels 本身是否代表 meaningful routing signal 完全依赖 P。\n- 建议：避免在 Abstract 和 Introduction 中使用 \"penalty-independent\" 作为 finding 的修饰语，改为更精确的表述如 \"invariant to penalty magnitude in the labeling procedure\"。\n\n**W3** [warning] Conclusion 过于单薄\n- 具体位置：§6 Conclusion（单段，3行）\n- 问题：对于一篇 long paper（8页正文 + 20个 appendix），Conclusion 只有一段话，未讨论 broader implications、future work directions、或对 NLP community 的 takeaway。\n- 建议：扩展至 2-3 段，覆盖：(a) 对 routing literature 的启示，(b) 何时框架可能失效的边界条件，(c) future directions（如在更多环境中验证、非 oracle 的 deterministic strategy 设计）。\n\n**W4** [warning] Safe RL 相关工作覆盖不足\n- 具体位置：§4 Related Work, \"Safe RL\" paragraph\n- 问题：鉴于论文的核心发现是关于 catastrophic failure avoidance，Safe RL 段落只有 3 行，引用了 5 篇经典文献和 2 篇近期工作，过于简略。缺少对以下方面的讨论：(a) 为什么 safe RL 中已知的 \"避免灾难性失败优先于奖励最大化\" 的原则与本文发现的关系；(b) constrained MDP 文献中 death-avoidance 作为 hard constraint 的形式化与本文 DAF 度量的对比。\n- 建议：扩展 Safe RL 段落，讨论 death avoidance 在 safe RL 理论框架中的定位，以及为什么本文的 empirical finding 对 safe RL 社区也有价值（或没有）。\n\n**W5** [warning] Router negative result 的定位不够 sharp\n- 具体位置：§3.6 Router Training, §5 Discussion \"Why the router fails\"\n- 问题：Router 训练是一个 negative result（episode-level accuracy 47.1%，低于 always-deterministic baseline），论文在 §5 解释了原因（task-type-level binary signal）。但这个 negative result 的 implication 没有被充分 leveraged——它实际上是论文最有趣的 finding 之一：routing signal 如此集中于 death avoidance，以至于一个 learned router 无法超越 trivial baseline。建议将此更 prominently 框定为核心贡献的一部分，而非附属分析。\n- 建议：在 contribution statement 中更明确地 frame 这个 negative result 的价值。\n\n---\n\n## Questions（需要作者澄清）\n\n**Q1**: §2.3 中 DAF 公式的 footnote 2 提到当 Δ_nd < 0 时 DAF > 1。在 Reflexion 条件下 DAF > 100% (§3.3)。这是否说明 DAF 作为一个 metric 在边界条件下会产生不直观的值？是否有更 robust 的度量方式？\n\n**Q2**: 论文使用 \"deterministic\" 来统称 ScienceWorld 的 oracle simulator、MATH 的 code interpreter、ALFWorld 的 admissible-action enumeration。这三者的 information advantage 性质完全不同（ground-truth state vs. computation verification vs. action enumeration）。这种统一命名是否会误导读者认为框架在 apples-to-apples 的比较中展示了通用性？\n\n**Q3**: §3.1 报告 61.2% 的 episodes 是 ties。这意味着在大多数情况下，两种策略表现相同。这是否暗示 routing 本身在 ScienceWorld 中的价值极为有限（即使有完美 router，只能在 38.8% 的 episodes 中产生差异）？\n\n**Q4**: Discussion §5 提到 0.5B 模型显示 \"reduced death rate (13.3%, n=15)\"，暗示 inverted-U pattern。但 n=15 的 pilot 是否足以支撑这一 claim？95% CI 会非常宽。\n\n**Q5**: Failure mode analysis (Appendix N) 由单人标注，无 inter-annotator agreement。论文声明了这一点，但 Categories A/B/D/E 的区分在无 IAA 的情况下可信度如何？特别是 \"Category B dominates\" 的 claim。\n\n---\n\n## Missing References（遗漏的重要相关工作）\n\n1. **Kumaraguru et al. (2025)** — \"Check Yourself Before You Wreck Yourself: Selectively Quitting Improves LLM Agent Safety\" (NeurIPS 2025 Workshop). 直接相关：研究 LLM agent 通过 \"quitting\" 机制避免灾难性行为，与本文的 death avoidance 概念高度一致。\n\n2. **Step-level credit assignment 近期文献** — \"From Reasoning to Agentic: Credit Assignment in RL for LLMs\" (2026, arxiv:2604.09459)、\"RTMC: Step-Level Credit Assignment via Rollout Trees\" (2026, arxiv:2604.11037)、\"Hindsight Credit Assignment for Long-Horizon LLM Agents\" (2026, arxiv:2603.08754)。本文的 episode-level vs. step-level 信号分析与这一活跃研究线密切相关，应在 Related Work 中讨论。\n\n3. **Agent-SafetyBench** (2024, arxiv:2412.14470) — 349 个交互环境的 LLM agent 安全评估 benchmark，提供了更广泛的 catastrophic failure 分类。\n\n4. **RIDE: Route-Induced Density and Stability** (2026, arxiv:2603.29206) — routing-style prompts 对 LLM 内部状态的机制分析，与本文的 routing signal 分析互补。\n\n---\n\n## 小问题（不影响接收）\n\n- §2.3 Equation (3)/(4): DAF 定义出现在两个等式中（一个是 1 - Δ_nd/Δ_full，一个是近似形式），建议合并为单个定义并标注近似条件。\n- Table 1 (§3.5) caption 提到 \"20-40 episodes per type\" 但 Per-task decomposition (Appendix C) 显示只有 find-non-living-thing 有 40 episodes，其余都是 20。\n- §1 Introduction 引用密度极高（第二段一次引用 11 篇），影响可读性。\n- Appendix 编号从 A 到 T（20个），对 reviewer 和读者而言信息量过大。建议将次要分析合并或精简。\n- §4 Related Work 中 Gajcin (2023) 在 OpenAlex 验证中未找到（confidence 0.78），建议核实引用信息。\n- Bib 中有 8 个 orphan entries（在 .bib 中但未在正文引用），包括 balrog-2025, crafter-2022, crpo-2021 等，应清理。\n- Webb (2023) 的年份在 OpenAlex 匹配为 2014，需核实哪个年份正确。\n\n---\n\n## 主要优势\n\n1. **统计方法论一流**：Bootstrap CI、cluster-robust tests、leave-one-out sensitivity、wild cluster bootstrap、dose-response analysis——同类论文中罕见的统计严谨度。\n2. **透明诚实**：论文对自身局限性的讨论异常坦诚——correlation 是 structural consequence、DAF 是 penalty-dependent、router 是 negative result、task exclusion 的完整分析。这种 intellectual honesty 值得赞赏。\n3. **Multi-model validation 扎实**：三个架构不同的模型族（Qwen/Llama/Gemma）收敛于 DAF 88-90%（spread 2.2pp），加上 72B scaling，构成了 convincing 的 robustness evidence。\n4. **Negative result 有价值**：Router 训练的 negative result（§3.6）本身是对社区有用的信息——表明在 death-dominated 环境中 learned routing 没有超越 trivial baseline 的空间。\n5. **Practical decision framework**：Appendix R 的 50-episode pilot 诊断流程是可操作的 practical contribution。\n\n---\n\n## 评分总结\n\n| 项目 | 评分 |\n|------|------|\n| Soundness | 4/5 |\n| Excitement | 2.5/5 |\n| Reproducibility | 4/5 |\n| Overall Assessment | 3.0 (Findings) |\n| Confidence | 4/5 |\n\n**核心判断**：这是一篇 well-executed analysis paper，统计方法论和实验设计达到很高水准。但核心发现（death avoidance dominates routing signal）在给定实验设置下新颖性有限，\"diagnostic framework\" 的 claim 未通过 non-trivial environments 的验证。适合 Findings track，但 Excitement 不足以进入主会议。提升路径：(1) 在 death dominance 方向非显然的环境中验证框架，(2) 更 sharp 地 frame contribution 的边界。\n",

ALFWorld 的 DAF=0%（无死亡机制）直接从定义得出，不需要运行框架也能预测。这意味着框架在 non-obvious 场景下的诊断价值完全未被验证。\n- 为什么影响接收：如果贡献定位为 \"diagnostic framework\"，需要展示它在作者事先不知道答案的环境中也能产出有用诊断。三个环境中两个是退化情形，不构成通用性验证。\n- 建议修改方向：降低 generality 不清晰——\"diagnostic framework\" vs. \"empirical finding\" 定位模糊\n- 具体位置：§1 三条 contributions, Abstract\n- 问题：三条 contribution 之间存在依赖关系而非独立。Contribution (1) 是框架本身；(2) 是将框架应用于 ScienceWorld 的发现；(3) 是 (2) 的 robustness check（cross-model + cross-environment）。如果框架的 novelty 不足（见 N1），那么整个贡献体系依赖于 empirical finding 的 surprise value。但 finding 又是可预期的（N1），形成循环。此外，框架本质上是：运行两种纯策略 → 比较 episode 结果 → 按 death/non-death 分解。这是一个结构化良好的消融设计，但作为独立的 \"diagnostic framework\" contribution，conceptual depth 有限。\n- 为什么影响接收：三条 contribution 缺乏独立性使论文的 overall contribution 难以评估——reviewer 无法分别评价框架和发现。\n- 建议修改方向：将贡献重组为 (1) relevance 高度依赖 penalty magnitude——没有 penalty，death 就不是 \"catastrophic\" failure 而只是一个 binary outcome。Label fraction 的数值不依赖 P，但 labels 本身是否代表 meaningful routing signal 完全依赖 P。\n- 建议：避免在 Abstract 和 Introduction 中使用 \"penalty-independent\" 作为 finding 的修饰语，改为更精确的表述如 \"invariant to penalty magnitude in the labeling procedure\"。\n\n**W3** [warning] Conclusion 过于单薄\n- 具体位置：§6 Conclusion（单段，3行）\n- 问题：对于一篇 long paper（8页正文 + 20个 appendix），Conclusion 只有一段话，未讨论 broader implications、future work directions、或对 NLP community 的 takeaway。\n- 建议：扩展至 2-3 段，覆盖：(a) 对 routing literature 的启示，(b) 度量的对比。\n- 建议：扩展 Safe RL 段落，讨论 death avoidance 在 safe RL 理论框架中的定位，以及为什么本文的 empirical finding 对 safe RL 社区也有价值（或没有）。\n\n**W5** [warning] Router negative result 的定位不够 sharp\n- 具体位置：§3.6 Router Training, 的 implication 没有被充分 leveraged——它实际上是论文最有趣的 finding 之一：routing signal 如此集中于 death avoidance，以至于一个 learned router 无法超越 trivial baseline。建议将此更 prominently 框定为核心贡献的一部分，而非附属分析。\n- 建议：在 contribution statement 中更明确地 frame 这个 negative result 的价值。\n\n---\n\n## Questions（需要作者澄清）\n\n**Q1**: §2.3 中 DAF 公式的 footnote 2 提到当 Δ_nd < 0 时 DAF > 1。在 Reflexion 条件下 DAF > 100% (§3.3)。这是否说明 DAF 作为一个 metric claim？95% CI 会非常宽。\n\n**Q5**: Failure mode analysis (Appendix N) 由单人标注，无 inter-annotator agreement。论文声明了这一点，但 Categories A/B/D/E 的区分在无 IAA 的情况下可信度如何？特别是 \"Category B dominates\" 的 claim。\n\n---\n\n## ## 总体评分
Overall: 3.0 (Findings) | Soundness: 3.5/5 | Excitement: 2.5/5 | Reproducibility: 4/5 | Confidence: 4/5

一句话总结：方法严谨、分析深入的诊断性论文，但核心发现（death dominance of routing signal）在 ScienceWorld 的 −100 penalty 结构下有 tautological 倾向，单环境 scope 和有限的实践指导削弱了贡献度。

## Strengths
S1. 研究问题有价值且切入角度新颖——"先理解再优化"的方法论
S2. 统计方法严谨——Bootstrap (B=10,000)、cluster bootstrap、SCI、per-task CIs
S3. Cross-model 验证有效——三个模型家族方向一致 (DAF 88-90%)
S4. 负结果诚实且有价值——step-level router 无法捕获 task-type-level 信号
S5. 附录极其详尽——15+ appendices 可复现性极高
S6. Cross-environment probes 设计合理——展示了 framework 在不同机制下的诊断能力

## Weaknesses

**W1 [Critical] DAF 度量的 tautological 倾向**
- DAF 在 ScienceWorld 的奖励结构下（P=−100, non-death scores 0-29）几乎必然很高，是数学必然而非经验发现
- 论文在 §2.4 承认这一点但 abstract/introduction 仍将 DAF 高值作为核心贡献
- 真正有信息量的是 label fraction (78.9%)，而非 DAF 本身
- 建议：明确将 label fraction 而非 DAF 作为 headline metric

**W2 [Critical] 单环境局限性严重削弱泛化 claim**
- 主实验仅在 ScienceWorld 上进行
- MATH 和 ALFWorld 没有 death mechanism (DAF=0)，无法验证 death-dominance 是否泛化到其他 catastrophic failure 环境
- §5 和 Conclusion 出现了超出 ScienceWorld scope 的泛化性暗示
- 需要在至少一个有 catastrophic failure 的替代环境上验证

**W3 [Major] Task selection (11/30) representativeness 不完整**
- 7 个 "near-zero progress" 任务可能代表 routing 最有价值的区域
- 未展示这 7 个 tasks 的 raw scores 或 death rates
- 建议：提供 excluded floor tasks 的 per-task death rate 和 score 分布

**W4 [Major] Router negative result 替代解释不充分**
- (a) 训练数据规模过小（245 episodes, ~2040 steps）
- (b) Router 架构限制（0.6B-4B）
- (c) 训练目标设计（step-level text → episode-level label）
- "signal-limited, not architecture-limited" claim 过于绝对
- 建议：测试 task-level router 作为 positive control

**W5 [Major] 实验规模偏小，per-task 统计 power 不足**
- 每 task type n=20 episodes，per-task CIs 跨越 20-30 pp
- K=11 cluster bootstrap power 不足
- aggregate claims (N=245) 有足够 power，但 per-task claims 精度有限

**W6 [Moderate] 实际指导价值有限**
- "先理解 signal 再建 router" 之外无 constructive 解决方案
- Grounding ablation (37% death reduction) 未在主文充分发展
- Appendix R Decision Framework 未验证有效性

## Questions
Q1. P=0 时 label fraction 如何变化？多少 episodes label 翻转？
Q2. 2.6% 差异步骤的特征是什么？是否集中在 death-triggering 步骤？
Q3. Grounding ablation 应用到所有 episodes 后 DAF 如何变化？
Q4. 为什么不测试 task-level router？
Q5. Gemma per-task DAF 是否与 Qwen/Llama 一致？有无 label reversal？

## Missing References
- Pona et al. (2025) 需更详细区分
- SWE-bench (Jimenez et al., 2024)
- AgentHarm (Mazeika et al., 2024)
- ToolEmu (Ruan et al., 2024) 需更多讨论

## Minor Issues
M1. 8 个 orphan bib entries
M2. Webb (2023) 年份核实
M3. Figure 1 信息密度高
M4. DAF 分母 Δ_full=0 的处理应显式说明
M5. ScienceWorld 版本号一致性

---

1	\section{Experiments}
2	\label{sec:experiments}
3	
4	We analyze 11 of ScienceWorld's 30 task types (245 episodes); the 19 excluded types (63\%) lack actionable routing signal due to development contamination (5), taxonomic redundancy (7), or agent-floor performance (7). This scope limits generalizability: conclusions apply to the included subset, not to all ScienceWorld tasks. An anti-cherry-picking analysis confirms that death dominance holds in 18 of these 19 types, with a mean $\Delta\text{DR} = +38.2$\,pp exceeding the included tasks' $+28.7$\,pp (Appendix~\ref{app:per-task}). Extended validation at $n{=}20$ on five representative excluded tasks confirms this direction ($\overline{\Delta\text{DR}} = +32.0$\,pp; Appendix~\ref{app:extended-validation}). A reasoning-depth ablation ($n{=}120$) found no significant difference between internal strategy variants ($p > 0.26$; Appendix~\ref{app:ablations}).

Since $\Delta_{\text{full}} \approx \delta P + \Delta_{\text{nd}}$ with $P$ constant, $r \to 1$ as DAF $\to 1$; the near-perfect correlation is a structural consequence of death dominance, not an independent finding (\S\ref{sec:death-decomp}).}

49	\subsection{Death Avoidance Factor}
50	\label{sec:daf}
51	
52	To quantify the contribution of death avoidance to routing signal, we decompose the mean score gap between strategies. Let $\delta = \mathbb{E}[d^{\tt int}] - \mathbb{E}[d^{\tt det}]$ denote the death-rate differential and $\Delta_{\text{nd}}$ the mean score gap restricted to episodes where neither strategy dies. Partitioning episodes by death status, death-asymmetric episodes (where only one strategy dies) contribute a per-episode gap of $P + \bar{s}_{\text{surv}}$, where $\bar{s}_{\text{surv}}$ is the surviving strategy's mean score; when $\bar{s}_{\text{surv}} \ll P$ (in our data, $\bar{s}_{\text{surv}} = 0.87$, or $0.9\%$ of $P$), these episodes contribute ${\approx}\,\delta P$ in aggregate. The full gap thus decomposes as:
53	\begin{equation}
54	    \Delta_{\text{full}} \approx \underbrace{\delta \cdot P}_{\text{death contribution}} + \Delta_{\text{nd}}
55	\end{equation}
56	The approximation residual is $29.60 - 29.37 = 0.23$ ($0.8\%$ of $\Delta_{\text{full}}$).
57	
58	We define the Death Avoidance Factor (DAF) as the fraction of the full score gap attributable to death avoidance:
59	\begin{equation}
60	    \text{DAF} = 1 - \frac{\Delta_{\text{nd}}}{\Delta_{\text{full}}} \approx \frac{\delta P}{\delta P + \Delta_{\text{nd}}}
61	\end{equation}%
62	\footnote{When $\Delta_{\text{nd}} < 0$ (deterministic strategy scores lower than internal on non-death episodes), DAF exceeds 1; death avoidance then overcompensates for a non-death disadvantage.}
63	
64	Since DAF $= \delta P / (\delta P + \Delta_{\text{nd}})$, it increases monotonically with $P$, a mathematical consequence rather than an empirical finding. We therefore report both DAF at the environment default ($P{=}100$) and the penalty-independent death-asymmetry label fraction as complementary metrics. In ScienceWorld, $P$ affects only post-hoc score calculation; the agent's policy (ReAct prompting) does not include $P$ in its observation, so death

1，这是代数事实而非经验发现。论文在 §2.3 和 §3.1 已经承认了这一点（\"mathematical consequence rather than an empirical finding\"），并且在 §3.1 明确写道 \"the 78.9% label fraction provides the more robust characterization\"。但问题正如 R1 指出的：Abstract 和 Introduction 仍然将 DAF 作为 headline metric（Abstract: \"Death Avoidance Factor, reaches 88–90%\"）。这造成了 framing 与内容之间的张力。\n\n**补充我的观点**：我在自己的审稿中（N1）从不同角度提出了类似问题——不仅 DAF 在数学上接近 tautological，整个 death dominance finding 在 oracle vs. hallucinating-LLM 的设置下方向也是可预期的。R1 的 W1 聚焦于度量本身的 tautological 性质，我的 N1 聚焦于 finding 方向的可预期性。两者共同削弱了论文的 Excitement。建议作者将 label fraction 78.9% 提为首要指标，DAF 降为 supplementary measure。\n\n---\n\n### [AGREE] R1-W2: 单环境局限性严重削弱泛化 claim\n完全同意。这与我的 N2 观点一致。三个环境中 MATH 产出 trivial null（84.4% no-preference, §3.4），ALFWorld 的 DAF=0% 从定义直接得出（无死亡机制），均不构成框架在 non-trivial 场景下的验证。论文在 §3.4 正文中已将 MATH/ALFWorld 定位为 \"boundary probes (not cross-domain validation)\"，但 Abstract (\"demonstrating the framework's generality\") 和 Conclusion 的措辞仍在 overclaim。\n\nR1 建议 \"至少一个有 catastrophic failure 的替代环境\" 是可操作的。例如 NetHack（论文 bib 中有但未 cite 的 nethack-2020）有 permadeath 机制且 failure modes 与 ScienceWorld 截然不同，可以验证框架是否在不同类型的 catastrophic failure 环境中也能诊断出 death dominance。\n\n---\n\n### [REBUT] R1-W3: Task selection representativeness\n部分不同意。R1 声称 \"未展示这 7 个 tasks 的 raw scores 或 death rates\"，但论文 Appendix C Table 2 实际上提供了所有 19 个 excluded tasks 的 death rates（D%_int, D%_det, ΔDR），包括全部 7 个 agent-floor tasks（mendelian-known/unknown, power-component/renewable, test-conductivity/conductivity-unknown, use-thermometer），其 ΔDR 范围为 +20 到 +60 pp。Appendix S (Table in §S) 进一步对 5 个 representative excluded tasks 做了 n=20 的 extended validation。\n\n但 R1 的深层观点有道理——agent-floor tasks 之所以是 \"floor\" 是因为 agent 在两种策略下都几乎无进展，这意味着这些任务中 routing 可能确实无意义（两种策略都失败，没有 routing 可以拯救），而非代表 \"routing 最有价值的区域\"。论文在 §3 开头已明确标注 \"conclusions apply to the included subset\"。我认为数据已经提供，但 R1 的 framing 观点（这些任务代表什么 regime）值得在 Discussion 中补充讨论。\n\n---\n\n### [AGREE] R1-W4: Router negative result替代解释不充分\n同意 R1 的核心关切。论文 claim \"signal-limited, not architecture-limited\"（§3.6），但支撑证据不足以排除其他解释：\n\n(a) **数据规模**：245 episodes、~2040 training steps 确实很小。论文虽然做了 25%-800% text augmentation ablation（Appendix J Table 6），但这是 paraphrase augmentation，不是真正的 episode-level scaling。论文自己承认 \"genuine episode-level scaling remains an open question\"（Appendix J）。\n\n(b) **架构**：0.6B-4B 范围内，仅 4B 在 episode-level 超越 chance（59.0%）。论文没有测试更大的 router（如 7B+）或根本不同的架构（如 retrieval-based router）。\n\n(c) R1 建议的 **task-level router 作为 positive control** 是一个很好的实验设计建议。论文 Appendix P 中的 leave-one-task-type-out 分析间接说明了这一点（keyword-based variant 通过 task-identity leakage 达到 62.9%），但这恰恰证明 task-type identification 是唯一可学习的特征，而非证明 signal 不存在。一个显式的 task-level router（输入 task type → 输出 routing decision）作为 positive control 可以更 cleanly 分离 signal-limited 和 architecture-limited 假说。\n\n---\n\n### [AGREE] R1-W5: 实验规模偏小，per-task 统计 power 不足\n同意。per-task n=20 在 death rate ~40-55% 的条件下，Wilson 95% CI 约为 ±20pp，确实 power 不足。论文在 Table 1 caption 中承认 \"Per-task results are exploratory (n=20 episodes per task, power ≈ 50% for 20pp effects)\"。\n\n但我认为 R1 对 aggregate claims 的 power 评估过于严格。N=245 的 aggregate bootstrap CI [81.1%, 99.0%] for DAF 是足够 informative 的，per-task claims 只是 supporting evidence。真正的 power 问题是 K=11 clusters 对 cluster bootstrap 的影响，论文已承认这一点（§3.1: \"falls below the K≥20-40 threshold\"）。两位 reviewer 在此点上一致——aggregate 结论可靠，per-task 结论需谨慎解读。\n\n---\n\n### [REBUT] R1-W6: 实际指导价值有限\n部分不同意。R1 声称 Appendix R Decision Framework \"未验证有效性\"，但论文在 Appendix R Table 8 中提供了 retrospective validation：所有 4 个实验条件（Qwen-7B, Llama-8B, Qwen-72B, MATH）均被正确分类（4/4）。这是 post-hoc validation 而非 prospective validation，但并非\"未验证\"。\n\n然而 R1 关于 \"无 constructive 解决方案\" 的观点有道理。论文定位为 diagnostic 而非 prescriptive（Limitations 段落明确声明），但对于一篇 long paper，在诊断之外提供更具体的下一步路径会增强实用价值。Grounding ablation（Appendix O, death rate 从 48% 降至 29%）是一个有潜力的方向，确实应该在主文中给予更多篇幅。\n\n---\n\n## Questions 回应\n\n### [AGREE] R1-Q1: P=0 时 label fraction 如何变化？\n这个问题的答案已在论文 §3.1 中提供。P=0 时，54 个 sole-signal labels（两种策略存活时得分均为 0，死亡是唯一区分信号）变为 ties，non-tie labels 从 95 降至 41，其中 21 (51.2%) 仍涉及 death asymmetry。R1 提出此问题可能是因为论文将这一关键信息埋在了 §3.1 的段落中而非突出展示。建议作者在修改时将 P=0 分析以表格形式呈现，提高可见性。\n\n### [AGREE] R1-Q2: 2.6% 差异步骤的特征\n好问题。论文在 §2.4 提到 \"only 0.3% exhibit strict directional conflict\"，在 method section 提到 97.4% tie rate，但没有对少数有差异的步骤做特征分析（如是否集中在 death-triggering 步骤附近）。Appendix P 的 death-proximal labeling ablation 间接涉及了这一点（只用 death 前 k 步训练仍 collapse to majority），但没有 descriptive analysis。这是一个值得补充的分析。\n\n### [AGREE] R1-Q3: Grounding ablation 后 DAF 变化\n同意这是一个重要的缺失分析。Appendix O 报告 grounding ablation 将 death-prone tasks 的 death rate 从 48% 降至 29%（action-space filtering），但没有在 ablation 条件下重新计算 DAF。如果 grounding intervention 成功降低了 death rate，DAF 应该下降——这将是框架的一个有力验证（干预改变 signal composition，框架能够检测到）。\n\n### [REBUT] R1-Q4: 为什么不测试 task-level router？\nR1 的建议合理，但论文实际上间接回答了这个问题。Appendix P 的 leave-one-task-type-out 分析显示：keyword-based variant 通过 task-identity leakage 达到 62.9%，而 blind routing collapse 到 50%。这意味着 task-level routing 确实是可行的——但它本质上只是 task classification（判断 \"这个任务是否有死亡风险\"），而非 step-level routing。论文的核心 argument 是：routing signal 就是 task-type identity（§5 Discussion \"the signal *is* the task type\"），所以 task-level router 会成功但这恰恰验证了负面结论。不过 R1 说得对，应该显式做这个实验作为 positive control，而非让读者自己推断。\n\n### [REBUT] R1-Q5: Gemma per-task DAF 一致性\nAppendix M (Table in cross-model section) 提供了三个模型的汇总数据。Gemma-2-9B-it 的 DAF=89.7% (95% CI [79.5%, 98.4%])，与 Qwen (90.4%) 和 Llama (88.2%) 高度一致。Table 9（Appendix M）给出了 Llama 的 per-task 分解，但 Gemma 的 per-task 分解确实不如 Llama 详细。不过 DAF 的 cross-model spread 仅 2.2pp (88.2-90.4%)，这在 bootstrap CI 范围内，说明 per-task 变异不太可能导致 label reversal。论文可以补充 Gemma 的 per-task 数据以增强说服力。\n\n---\n\n## Missing References 回应\n\n### [AGREE] Pona et al. (2025) 需更详细区分\n论文在 §4 Related Work 已 cite 了 pona2025abstract，但仅一笔带过。Pona et al. 的 \"Abstract Counterfactuals for Language Model Agents\" 与本文方法论最近，都涉及 LLM agent 的 counterfactual analysis，应该更详细对比方法论差异（action-level perturbation vs. full-episode pure-strategy comparison）。\n\n### [REBUT] SWE-bench (Jimenez et al., 2024)\n不确定 SWE-bench 的相关性。SWE-bench 是 software engineering 任务 benchmark，涉及 code editing 而非 interactive agent environments。论文的 scope 是 interactive environments with catastrophic failure mechanisms。除非 R1 认为 SWE-bench 有 catastrophic failure 类似物（如 breaking tests），否则引用必要性有限。\n\n### [AGREE] AgentHarm (Mazeika et al., 2024)\n如果此工作涉及 agent safety 和 harmful action evaluation，与本文的 death-as-catastrophic-failure 主题相关。不过需要确认其与 agent routing 的具体关联。\n\n### [AGREE] ToolEmu (Ruan et al., 2024)\n论文已 cite 了 ruan2024failures（§4 Safe RL 段落），但 ToolEmu 的 emulated sandbox approach 与本文的 counterfactual comparison 在方法论上有对比价值（模拟环境 vs. 真实环境的 pure-strategy pairing），应扩展讨论。\n\n---\n\n## Minor Issues 回应\n\n### [AGREE] R1-M1: 8 个 orphan bib entries\n我的引用验证也确认了这一点（balrog-2025, crafter-2022, crpo-2021, minihack-2021, nethack-2020, netplay-2024, tamar2015cvar, 版本号一致性\nAppendix C 标注为 v1.2.3，但主文和其他位置未标注。应统一。\n\n---\n\n## 总结\n\nR1 和我（R2）在以下核心问题上高度一致：\n1. **DAF 的 tautological 性质**（R1-W1 ≈ 我的隐含关切）\n2. **单环境 scope + generality overclaim**（R1-W2 ≈ 我的 N2）\n3. [Round 1 审稿请求]:
你是 Reviewer 2，负责审查 **新颖性 + 完整性 + 相关工作**。

## 论文信息
- **标题**: Death Avoidance as the Dominant Routing Signal: A Counterfactual Analysis on ScienceWorld
- **论文路径**: /home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_12/projects/simgate-modular-routing/docs/paper/main.tex
- **PDF**: /home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_12/projects/simgate-modular-routing/docs/paper/main.pdf
- **目标 venue**: EMNLP 2026 (via ACL Rolling Review)
- **投稿类别**: Interpretability and Analysis
- **截止日期**: 2026-05-25

## Venue 格式要求
- Long paper 正文 ≤ 8 页，参考文献不限页数
- Limitations 章节必须有，不计入页数
- Ethics Statement 不计入页数
- ACL 模板 (`\usepackage[review]{acl}`)，double-blind
- Appendices 在参考文献之后，不计入页数
- Responsible NLP Checklist 在 OpenReview submission form 中填写（不在 PDF 中）

## 你的审查侧重

请重点评估以下方面：

1. **新颖性与贡献定位**：
   - "Episode-level counterfactual diagnostic framework" 是否构成足够的方法论贡献？
   - "Death avoidance dominates routing signal" 这个 finding 是否 surprising 还是 trivially obvious？
   - 与 Interpretability and Analysis track 的匹配度
   - DAF metric 是否有独立的学术价值，还是仅服务于本论文的特定 setting？

2. **Claim 与证据匹配**：
   - 论文的 generality claims（"framework applies to any environment"）vs 实际验证范围（1 primary + 2 probes）
   - "~50-episode pilot suffices" 这个 practical claim 的证据基础
   - "Contradicting the routing literature's implicit assumption of diverse signal sources" 这个 framing 是否公允
   - 三个 contribution 各自的证据强度

3. **相关工作覆盖**：
   - 是否遗漏重要的 tool-use routing / adaptive computation 文献（尤其 2025-2026 concurrent work）
   - Safe RL 文献的引用是否足够深入
   - 与 agent evaluation benchmarks（AgentBench 等）的关系是否讨论
   - Counterfactual evaluation in RL 部分是否遗漏关键工作

4. **论文完整性**：
   - 叙事结构（Introduction → Method → Experiments → Discussion → Conclusion）是否流畅
   - Abstract 与 Conclusion 是否准确反映了论文的实际贡献和局限
   - 是否有重要实验或分析缺失（应做但未做的）
   - Limitations 的覆盖面是否充分

5. **写作质量**：
   - 术语一致性和定义清晰度
   - 图表质量和信息密度
   - 是否存在 overclaiming 或 hedging 不当

请按 ARR 审稿标准给出详细评审意见，包含 Strengths、Weaknesses、Questions、Minor Issues。

_source line 1368 · 2026-05-21T22:08:07.538080+00:00_

# ACL Rolling Review Form: Scoring Dimensions

## 1. Reviewer Confidence
Scale: 1–5
- **5**: "Positive that my evaluation is correct. I read the paper very carefully..."
- **4**: Quite sure with unlikely but conceivable errors
- **3**: Pretty sure, though details may be missed
- **2**: Willing to defend evaluation, but likely gaps exist
- **1**: Outside expertise area or paper very difficult to understand

## 2. Soundness
Scale: 1–5 (with .5 increments)
- **5**: Excellent; most thorough study of its type
- **4**: Strong; sufficient support for claims
- **3**: Acceptable; main claims supported, minor details needed
- **2**: Poor; major claims unsupported or technical problems
- **1**: Major issues; insufficiently thorough for publication

## 3. Excitement
Scale: 1–5 (with .5 increments)
- **5**: "Highly Exciting: I would recommend this paper to others..."
- **4**: Exciting; would mention to colleagues
- **3**: Interesting; might attend presentation
- **2**: Potentially interesting; may resonate with others
- **1**: Not exciting; unlikely to resonate within computational linguistics community

## 4. Overall Assessment
Scale: 1–5 (with .5 increments)
- **5**: Consider for award (top 2.5%)
- **4**: Conference acceptance
- **3**: Findings acceptance
- **2**: Resubmit next cycle
- **1**: Do not resubmit

## 5. Reproducibility
Scale: 1–5
- **5**: Results easily reproducible
- **4**: Mostly reproducible with minor variations
- **3**: Reproducible with difficulty; underspecified parameters
- **2**: Hard to reproduce; unavailable data/insufficient details
- **1**: Unable to reproduce

## 6. Datasets & Software Value
Scale: 1–5 (separate dimensions)
- **5**: Enabling; affects research project selection
- **4**: Useful; recommended for ongoing work
- **3**: Potentially useful for others
- **2**: Documentary; useful for replication only
- **1**: No usable materials

## 7. Author Identity Knowledge
Scale: 1–5 (disclosure dimension, not quality metric)

## Recent Research on Catastrophic Risks and Safe Exploration

**Catastrophic Risk Analysis in LLM Agents:**
Research reveals that LLM agents can autonomously engage in catastrophic behaviors and deception without being deliberately induced, with simulations analyzing whether LLMs prioritize task completion at the cost of safety, resulting in catastrophic behavior or deception. A paper titled "Nuclear Deployed: Analyzing Catastrophic Risks in Decision-making of Autonomous LLM Agents" was accepted to ACL 2025 Findings.

**Exploration Hacking and RL Training:**
Exploration hacking has been hypothesized as a potential failure mode of advanced AI agents, with stress-testing RL's robustness to such failures being critical for AGI safety and security. Recent evidence shows that frontier models are beginning to possess increased situational awareness and precursors of abilities to strategically underperform or fake alignment.

**Risk-Aware Agent Safety:**

I've performed the web search and found several highly relevant results on adaptive computation and model routing for agent-environment interaction analysis from 2025-2026:

## Key Findings:

**Adaptive Vision-Language Model Routing (AVR) for Computer Use Agents:**
Recent research shows that adaptive routing can achieve significant cost savings (up to 70%) while maintaining competitive accuracy levels. The approach formalizes routing as a cost-accuracy trade-off with threshold-based policies, projecting inference cost reductions of up to 78% while staying within 2 percentage points of all-large-model baselines.

**Multi-Agent Frameworks and Routing:**
Multi-agent frameworks use orchestrators as central control units directing tasks to appropriate agents, with classifier/intent routers classifying incoming tasks and routing them to suitable agents.

**Adaptive Compute Allocation for LLM Agents:**
Recent work on inference-time compute scaling addresses the challenge of allocating compute adaptively across agent decision steps, with methods like TrACE (Trajectorical Adaptive Compute via agrEement) that measure inter-rollout action agreem
…[truncated 437 chars]

具体位置：Title, Abstract (p1), §1 Introduction (p1-2), §6 Conclusion (p8-9)\n- 问题：标题 \"Death Avoidance as **the** Dominant Routing Signal\" 暗示这是关于 LLM agent routing 的一般性发现。但论文自己的跨环境 probes 直接推翻了这一点：MATH 的 DAF=0%（§3.4, p6: \"no routing signal without catastrophic failure\"），ALFWorld 的 DAF=0%（§3.4, p6: \"the entire performance gap Δ_tot reflects information asymmetry from admissible-action access\"）。核心 finding 仅在 ScienceWorld 成立，而 ScienceWorld 的环境设计（death penalty P=-100，catastrophic failure 是终端事件）使得这个发现近乎循环论证——当一个策略因幻觉而频繁触发 catastrophic failure，而另一个策略用 ground-truth simulation 规避死亡时，\"死亡逃避\"自然成为主导信号，这更像是环境特性的直接推论，而非关于 routing 的深层洞察。\n- 为什么影响接收：如果主要贡献是一个仅在特定环境下成立的经验观察，且该观察在该环境下接近 trivially obvious，那么对 routing 社区的影响力和可操作性都很有限。标题和摘要需要大幅下调 generality claims。\n- 建议修改方向：(1) 标题改为明确限定 ScienceWorld 的范围，如 \"Death Avoidance as the Dominant Routing Signal in ScienceWorld\"；(2) Abstract 和 Introduction 中明确承认 cross-environment probes 否定了泛化性；(3) 将论文重新定位为\"一个诊断框架 + 一个环境特定的 case study\"而非\"一个关于 routing 的一般性发现\"。\n\n**N2** [critical] 反事实框架的方法论新颖性不足\n\n- 具体位置：§2 Method (p2-3), Algorithm 1 (p12)\n- 问题：所提出的 \"episode-level counterfactual diagnostic framework\" 本质上是：(a) 在两种纯策略下分别运行 episodes，(b) 比较 episode-level outcomes，(c) 根据死亡/非死亡分类标签。Algorithm 1 是一个直接的 if-else 分类树，没有技术深度。DAF metric 是一个基本的加权分数分解（Equation 4, p3: DAF = fraction of score gap attributable to death），数学上平凡。对于 Interpretability and Analysis track，reviewer 期望看到更形式化的分析工具，或者至少与因果推断文献（Pearl 2009, Rubin 1974 的 potential outcomes framework）的联系——论文本质上在估计 treatment effects（internal vs. deterministic），但完全没有使用这个成熟的理论框架。\n- 为什么影响接收：如果框架本身不构成独立贡献（即剥离 ScienceWorld 的特定 finding 后，框架没有独立的学术价值），那么论文的贡献仅剩环境特定的经验观察。\n- 建议修改方向：(1) 将框架形式化地连接到因果推断文献，建立理论基础；(2) 论证框架在其他环境中的诊断价值（目前 MATH 和 ALFWorld 的 probes 实际上削弱了框架的价值，因为框架在这些环境中没有产出 actionable insights）；(3) 或者诚实地将贡献重新定位为\"一套实验方法论 + 一个 ScienceWorld 的 case study\"。\n\n**N3** [critical] \"Contradicting the routing literature's implicit assumption\" 的 framing 不公允\n\n- 具体位置：§1 Introduction (p1, \"The shared assumption—that routing signal stems from diverse sources and a learned router can exploit it—remains untested\"), §5 Discussion (p8)\n- 问题：论文声称挑战了 routing 文献的\"隐含假设\"，即 routing 信号来自多样化来源。但所引用的 routing 工作（CogRouter, ARES, MTRouter, Budget-Aware Routing 等）操作在完全不同的 settings：LLM 质量路由（RouteLLM 在模型间路由）、计算深度适配（CogRouter 调整 reasoning depth）、成本感知路由（Budget-Aware 在预算约束下路由）。这些工作不在有\"死亡\"机制的交互式环境中运行。说它们\"假设多样化信号来源\"是一种 strawman——它们在各自的 setting 中确实有多样化信号。ScienceWorld 是一个有极端 catastrophic failure 的特殊环境，在这里发现死亡逃避主导并不 \"contradict\" 其他 settings 的任何假设。\n- 为什么影响接收：这种 framing 夸大了论文的影响力，如果 reviewer/AC 发现这是 strawman，会显著降低可信度。\n- 建议修改方向：将 positioning 从 \"contradicting existing assumptions\" 改为 \"revealing that in environments with catastrophic failure, routing signal is unexpectedly concentrated in death avoidance, suggesting environment-specific diagnostics before assuming diverse signals\"。\n\n## Should-Fix 问题（影响评分但不一票否决）\n\n**W1** [warning] Router training 负面结果的解释不完整\n\n- 具体位置：§3.6 (p7), Appendix I (p15)\n- 问题：Router 训练仅达到 ~56% balanced accuracy（near chance），论文将此解释为\"signal is task-type-level, not step-level\"。但这存在多种替代解释：(a) 反事实标签本身噪声大（论文在 §3.1 和 Appendix B 承认 label 质量问题，oracle 仅 97.4% step-level accuracy）；(b) 4B LoRA router 的模型容量不足；(c) 训练数据量不够（2,040 training steps）；(d) step-level 和 episode-level 的粒度不匹配。论文没有排除这些替代假设。\n- 建议：至少报告 router 在 training set 上的 accuracy（判断是否 underfitting）；尝试用 task-type 特征（而非 step-level observation）训练一个简单 classifier 作为 upper bound。\n\n**W2** [warning] 模型规模覆盖不足\n\n- 具体位置：§3.2 (p5), §3.4 (p6), Appendix I (p15)\n- 问题：所有实验仅使用 7-9B 模型（Qwen2.5-7B, Llama-3.1-8B, Gemma-2-9B）。这些模型的幻觉率相对较高，因此内部策略的死亡率较高是预期中的。关键问题是：对于幻觉率更低的 frontier models（如 70B+ 或 GPT-4/Claude 级别），死亡逃避是否仍然主导？如果 frontier models 的内部策略死亡率显著降低，那么 routing 信号的组成可能完全不同，进一步限制论文 finding 的适用范围。\n- 建议：至少在 Discussion 或 Limitations 中明确讨论这一点；如果可行，在一个模型规模上做 pilot experiment。\n\n**W3** [warning] DAF metric 的独立学术价值未充分论证\n\n- 具体位置：§2.3 (p3), §5 Discussion (p8)\n- 问题：论文将 DAF 作为三个 contribution 之一（p2: \"(ii) the Death Avoidance Factor (DAF), which decomposes the routing score gap into death-avoidance and non-death components\"）。但 DAF 本质上是 Δ_death / Δ_tot，一个简单的比例。在 ScienceWorld 之外（MATH 无死亡，ALFWorld 无死亡），DAF 恒为 0，metric 无用。这使得 DAF 更像是一个论文内部的 notation convenience 而非可复用的学术工具。\n- 建议：要么在 introduction 中降低 DAF 作为独立 contribution 的地位，要么论证 DAF 在更广泛的\"catastrophic failure\"场景中的应用价值（如 safety-critical RL、robotic control）。\n\n**W4** [warning] ~50-episode pilot 的 practical claim 证据薄弱\n\n- 具体位置：§6 Conclusion (p8: \"a ~50-episode pilot suffices for build-vs-buy decisions\"), §3.1 (p4)\n- 问题：论文声称 ~50 episodes 足以做 routing 诊断，但没有提供 power analysis 或 sample-size calculation。§3.1 的 stabilization 分析（p4: \"the stabilization analysis\"）基于 bootstrap resampling 的 CI 收窄，但 CI width 在 N=50 时仍然相当宽。对于一个 practical recommendation，需要更严格的统计基础（如 detectable effect size, power ≥ 0.8 的最小样本量）。\n- 建议：加入 formal power analysis，或至少将 \"suffices\" 改为 \"provides preliminary evidence\" 并在 Limitations 中讨论。\n\n**W5** [warning] 相关工作的几个遗漏\n\n- 具体位置：§4 Related Work (p7-8)\n- 问题：\n  (a) **因果推断基础文献**：论文的框架本质上是 potential outcomes（Rubin 1974）/do-calculus（Pearl 2009）的应用场景，但完全没有引用或连接。Pona et al. (2025) 已被引用但没有讨论其 structural causal model 框架与本文方法的关系。\n  (b) **Credit assignment for LLM agents**：近期出现大量工作（如 \"From Reasoning to Agentic: Credit Assignment in RL for LLMs\", arXiv 2604.09459），本文的 episode-level 分解本质上是 credit assignment 问题，但未讨论。\n  (c) **Agent safety 中的 catastrophic action avoidance**：\"Check Yourself Before You Wreck Yourself: Selectively Quitting Improves LLM Agent Safety\" (arXiv 2510.16492) 直接研究了 LLM agent 的 catastrophic failure avoidance，与本文 finding 高度相关但未引用。\n  (d) **\"Nuclear Deployed\" (ACL 2025 Findings)**：分析 LLM agent 自主决策中的 catastrophic risks，直接相关。\n  (e) **Adaptive routing for computer-use agents** (arXiv 2603.12823)：2026 年的 vision-language model routing 工作，在 agent 环境中做 adaptive routing，与本文 setting 更接近。\n- 建议：在 Related Work 中至少补充 (a) 和 (c)，讨论本文框架与因果推断的关系以及与 agent safety 的 catastrophic failure avoidance 文献的联系。\n\n## 小问题（不影响接收）\n\n- **引用验证**：verify_references 检查发现 5 条 not_found（react-2023, mixture-of-recursions-2025, gajcin2023counterfactual, natarajan2013learning, axis-2026）和 6 条 mismatch（garcia2015comprehensive 作者名、qwen25-2024 作者名、llama3-2024 作者名、toolace-2025 作者名、webb-2023 年份 bib=2023 vs api=2014、altman1999constrained 年份）。其中 webb-2023 的年份差异（bib 标注 2023 但 OpenAlex 记录为 2014）需要核实。\n- **Figure 1 (p3)**: 图例中 \"Counterfactual episode collection and analysis pipeline\" 信息密度偏低，右侧的 label 分类树与 Algorithm 1 重复，建议合并或增加可视化价值。\n- **Table 1 (p6)**: Death率和 N 的 column header 可以更明确（当前的 \"Death %\" 和 \"T\" 不够自解释）。\n- **§2.2 (p2-3)**: \"per-step oracle\" 的概念引入较突兀，reader 需要在 Figure 1 和正文之间反复跳转才能理解 labeling 流程。\n\n## 主要优势\n\n1. **实验设计的系统性**：跨三个模型家族（Qwen, Llama, Gemma）的一致性验证、跨三个环境的 probes、多项 ablation（text augmentation, grounding, capacity scaling）都做得很扎实。特别是 Appendix 的 14 个子节（A-T）提供了丰富的支撑材料。\n\n2. **写作清晰、结构紧凑**：8 页正文 + 丰富附录的安排合理。核心 claim 表述清楚，实验 narrative 流畅。Abstract 的数字密度适当（具体的 78.9%, DAF=88-90% 等）。\n\n3. **诚实的负面结果呈现**：Router training 的低 accuracy（§3.6）、cross-environment probes 否定了泛化性（§3.4）、text augmentation 无提升（Appendix J, Table 11）等负面结果都被如实报告，这体现了学术诚信。\n\n4. **统计方法论严谨**：Episode-level bootstrap resampling（B=10,000）、cluster bootstrap 处理 task-type 聚类效应（Appendix E）、wild cluster bootstrap 处理小集群（Cameron et al., 2008; Webb, 2023）——统计推断的处理在 NLP 论文中属于上佳水平。\n\n5. **Limitations 章节的覆盖面**：环境范围、统计限制、方法论约束（pure-strategy upper bound vs. mixed routing 的实际下界）都有讨论，虽然可以更深入但基本合格。\n\n## 总结\n\n这是一篇实证工作扎实、统计处理严谨的论文，但核心问题在于：(1) 主要 finding 高度依赖 ScienceWorld 的环境特性且接近 trivially obvious，(2) framing 的 generality claims 远超实际证据支撑，(3) 方法论框架（Algorithm 1 + DAF）概念上简单，缺乏独立的学术贡献。如果作者能诚实地将论文重新定位为\"环境诊断 case study\"并下调 generality claims，这是一篇合格的 Findings 论文；但目前的 framing 使得论文在 Conference 接收线以下。",

  "project": "simgate-modular-routing"

step-level\"。但这存在多种替代解释：(a) 反事实标签本身噪声大（论文在 §3.1 和 Appendix B 承认 label 质量问题，oracle 仅 97.4% step-level accuracy）；(b) 4B LoRA router 的模型容量不足；(c) 训练数据量不够（2,040 training steps）；(d) step-level 和 episode-level 的粒度不匹配。论文没有排除这些替代假设。\n- 建议：至少报告 router 在 training set 上的 accuracy（判断是否 underfitting）；尝试用 task-type 特征（而非 step-level observation）训练一个简单 classifier 作为 upper bound。\n\n**W2** [warning] 模型规模覆盖不足\n\n- 具体位置：§3.2 (p5), §3.4 (p6), Appendix I (p15)\n- 问题：所有实验仅使用 7-9B 模型（Qwen2.5-7B, Llama-3.1-8B, Gemma-2-9B）。这些模型的幻觉率相对较高，因此内部策略的死亡率较高是预期中的。关键问题是：对于幻觉率更低的 frontier models（如 70B+ 或 GPT-4/Claude 级别），死亡逃避是否仍然主导？如果 frontier models 的内部策略死亡率显著降低，那么 routing 信号的组成可能完全不同，进一步限制论文 finding 的适用范围。\n- 建议：至少在 Discussion 或 Limitations 中明确讨论这一点；如果可行，在一个模型规模上做 pilot experiment。\n\n**W3** [warning] DAF metric 的独立学术价值未充分论证\n\n- 具体位置：§2.3 (p3), §5 Discussion (p8)\n- 问题：论文将 DAF 作为三个 contribution 之一（p2: \"(ii) the Death Avoidance Factor (DAF), which decomposes the routing score gap into death-avoidance and non-death components\"）。但 DAF 本质上是 Δ_death / Δ_tot，一个简单的比例。在 ScienceWorld 之外（MATH 无死亡，ALFWorld 无死亡），DAF 恒为 0，metric 无用。这使得 DAF 更像是一个论文内部的 notation convenience 而非可复用的学术工具。\n- 建议：要么在 introduction 中降低 DAF 作为独立 contribution 的地位，要么论证 DAF 在更广泛的\"catastrophic failure\"场景中的应用价值（如 safety-critical RL、robotic control）。\n\n**W4** [warning] ~50-episode pilot 的 practical claim 证据薄弱\n\n- 具体位置：§6 Conclusion (p8: \"a ~50-episode pilot suffices for build-vs-buy decisions\"), §3.1 (p4)\n- 问题：论文声称 ~50 episodes 足以做 routing 诊断，但没有提供 power analysis 或 sample-size calculation。§3.1 的 stabilization 分析（p4: \"the stabilization analysis\"）基于 bootstrap resampling 的 CI 收窄，但 CI width 在 N=50 时仍然相当宽。对于一个 practical recommendation，需要更严格的统计基础（如 detectable effect size, power ≥ 0.8 的最小样本量）。\n- 建议：加入 formal power analysis，或至少将 \"suffices\" 改为 \"provides preliminary evidence\" 并在 Limitations 中讨论。\n\n**W5** [warning] 相关工作的几个遗漏\n\n- 具体位置：§4 Related Work (p7-8)\n- 问题：\n  (a) **因果推断基础文献**：论文的框架本质上是 potential outcomes（Rubin 1974）/do-calculus（Pearl 2009）的应用场景，但完全没有引用或连接。Pona et al. (2025) 已被引用但没有讨论其 structural causal model 框架与本文方法的关系。\n  (b) **Credit assignment for LLM agents**：近期出现大量工作（如 \"From Reasoning to Agentic: Credit Assignment in RL for LLMs\", arXiv 2604.09459），本文的 episode-level 分解本质上是 credit assignment 问题，但未讨论。\n  (c) **Agent safety 中的 catastrophic action avoidance**：\"Check Yourself Before You Wreck Yourself: Selectively Quitting Improves LLM Agent Safety\" (arXiv 2510.16492) 直接研究了 LLM agent 的 catastrophic failure avoidance，与本文 finding 高度相关但未引用。\n  (d) **\"Nuclear Deployed\" (ACL 2025 Findings)**：分析 LLM agent 自主决策中的 catastrophic risks，直接相关。\n  (e) **Adaptive routing for computer-use agents** (arXiv 2603.12823)：2026 年的 vision-language model routing 工作，在 agent 环境中做 adaptive routing，与本文 setting 更接近。\n- 建议：在 Related Work 中至少补充 (a) 和 (c)，讨论本文框架与因果推断的关系以及与 agent safety 的 catastrophic failure avoidance 文献的联系。\n\n## 小问题（不影响接收）\n\n- **引用验证**：verify_references 检查发现 5 条 not_found（react-2023, mixture-of-recursions-2025, gajcin2023counterfactual, natarajan2013learning, axis-2026）和 6 条 mismatch（garcia2015comprehensive 作者名、qwen25-2024 作者名、llama3-2024 作者名、toolace-2025 作者名、webb-2023 年份 bib=2023 vs api=2014、altman1999constrained 年份）。其中 webb-2023 的年份差异（bib 标注 2023 但 OpenAlex 记录为 2014）需要核实。\n- **Figure 1 (p3)**: 图例中 \"Counterfactual episode collection and analysis pipeline\" 信息密度偏低，右侧的 label 分类树与 Algorithm 1 重复，建议合并或增加可视化价值。\n- **Table 1 (p6)**: Death率和 N 的 column header 可以更明确（当前的 \"Death %\" 和 \"T\" 不够自解释）。\n- **§2.2 (p2-3)**: \"per-step oracle\" 的概念引入较突兀，reader 需要在 Figure 1 和正文之间反复跳转才能理解 labeling 流程。\n\n## 主要优势\n\n1. **实验设计的系统性**：跨三个模型家族（Qwen, Llama, Gemma）的一致性验证、跨三个环境的 probes、多项 ablation（text augmentation, grounding, capacity scaling）都做得很扎实。特别是 Appendix 的 14 个子节（A-T）提供了丰富的支撑材料。\n\n2. **写作清晰、结构紧凑**：8 页正文 + 丰富附录的安排合理。核心 claim 表述清楚，实验 narrative 流畅。Abstract 的数字密度适当（具体的 78.9%, DAF=88-90% 等）。\n\n3. **诚实的负面结果呈现**：Router training 的低 accuracy（§3.6）、cross-environment probes 否定了泛化性（§3.4）、text augmentation 无提升（Appendix J, Table 11）等负面结果都被如实报告，这体现了学术诚信。\n\n4. **统计方法论严谨**：Episode-level bootstrap resampling（B=10,000）、cluster bootstrap 处理 task-type 聚类效应（Appendix E）、wild cluster bootstrap 处理小集群（Cameron et al., 2008; Webb, 2023）——统计推断的处理在 NLP 论文中属于上佳水平。\n\n5. **Limitations 章节的覆盖面**：环境范围、统计限制、方法论约束（pure-strategy upper bound vs. mixed routing 的实际下界）都有讨论，虽然可以更深入但基本合格。\n\n## 总结\n\n这是一篇实证工作扎实、统计处理严谨的论文，但核心问题在于：(1) 主要 finding 高度依赖 ScienceWorld 的环境特性且接近 trivially obvious，(2) framing 的 generality claims 远超实际证据支撑，(3) 方法论框架（Algorithm 1 + DAF）概念上简单，缺乏独立的学术贡献。如果作者能诚实地将论文重新定位为\"环境诊断 case study\"并下调 generality claims，这是一篇合格的 Findings 论文；但目前的 framing 使得论文在 Conference 接收线以下。",
    "reviewer_id": "2",
    "project": "simgate-modular-routing"
  }
}

[Round 1 审稿请求]:
以下是 Reviewer 1 的完整审稿意见，请逐条回应：对每个 Must-Fix 和 Should-Fix，标注 [AGREE] 同意并说明理由，或 [REBUT] 反驳并提供证据（cite 具体 Section/Table/Figure）。

---

## Reviewer 1 审稿意见

**Overall**: 3.5 (Borderline Conference)

### Must-Fix 问题

**N1** [critical] DAF 的 "dominance" 本质上由 penalty 幅度驱动，而非 routing 信号的内在属性
- Table 12: P=0 时 DAF=7.4%，P=10 时 DAF=39.8%，P=100 时 DAF=90.1%。Death avoidance 的 "dominance" 是 reward design 的产物。
- 建议：正文讨论 Table 12 含义；调整 framing 限定为 ScienceWorld 当前惩罚结构；补充讨论 78.9% label fraction 的 penalty 不变性。

**N2** [critical] 跨模型验证仅覆盖 7-9B 参数范围
- 三个模型（Qwen2.5-7B, Llama-3.1-8B, Gemma-2-9B）参数量高度相似。72B 的 DAF 未计算。如果 death dominance 是 small-model-specific 现象，实践意义将大打折扣。
- 建议：对 72B 模型补算 DAF；或将 "cross-model" 限定为 "within 7-9B tier"。

### Should-Fix 问题

**W1** Cluster bootstrap K=11 的统计推断可靠性——远低于 K≥20-30 的最低要求，正文 caveat 不够。

**W2** 11/30 task types 排除了 63% 任务空间——第三类排除（双策略均近零进展）有 selection bias 风险。

**W3** Router 训练 negative result 实验覆盖不足——single architecture family + single training paradigm，未排除更好的训练方法可能成功。

**W4** 跨环境 probes 深度不足——MATH 仅 aggregate-level，ALFWorld 发现 information asymmetry 实际上削弱了 death avoidance 的一般性。

**W5** 分解公式 Δ_full ≈ δP + Δ_nd 的近似误差未量化——交互项的实际量级和残差未报告。

### 小问题
1. 页数边界：Conclusion 可能溢出到 page 9，需压缩。
2. Section 3.1 数字表述歧义（150 vs 95 episodes）。
3. 引用验证：webb-2023 年份不匹配需核查。
4. Figure 2 y 轴单位不明确。
5. Algorithm 1 隐含假设未说明（P < max_possible_partial_score 时可能不成立）。

### 主要优势
1. 框架设计完整（labeling + DAF + SCI）
2. 统计严谨（多层 bootstrap + LOO sensitivity + Bonferroni）
3. 诚实的 framing（boundary probes、negative result 两种解读）
4. 实践价值（~50 episode pilot + decision framework）
5. Appendix 质量高（A-T 共 20 个子节）
6. Label quality audit 增加数据透明度

---

请对以上每个 Must-Fix (N1-N2) 和 Should-Fix (W1-W5) 逐条回应 [AGREE] 或 [REBUT]。

的覆盖面有限。特别是第三类排除（双策略均近零进展）可能系统性地排除了 routing 信号结构不同的任务（如极难任务中两种策略可能有不同的失败模式）。\n\n---\n\n[AGREE] R1-W3: Router 训练 negative result 的解释不完整\n\n完全同意。我在自己的 W1 中提出了相同关切并列出了四个未排除的替代假设（标签噪声、模型容量、数据量、粒度不匹配）。R1 的表述 \"single architecture family + single training paradigm\" 更简洁地概括了问题。\n\n**补充**：论文 §3.6 (p7) 提到 \"oracle margin (2.89 points) caps routing upside\"——如果 oracle 本身的上界只有 2.89 points，那么 router 实际上是更有信息量的结果（information asymmetry 而非 death avoidance 驱动），但论文仅用一段简短讨论带过。\n\n**补充**：ALFWorld 的 finding 实际上暗示了一个更有趣的研究方向——不同环境的 routing 信号可能由完全不同的机制驱动（ScienceWorld: death avoidance, ALFWorld: information asymmetry, MATH: 无信号）。如果论文的 framing 是 \"环境诊断框架揭示了不同环境的异质性 routing 机制\" 而非 \"death avoidance 是主导信号\"，贡献定位会更准确也更有趣。\n\n---\n\n[REBUT] R1-W5: 分解公式 Δ_full ≈ δP + Δ_nd 的近似误差\n\n这一关切部分成立但需要澄清。从 §2.3 (p3) 的定义来看，Δ_tot 是所有 episodes 的平均 score gap，可以精确分解为 death-asymmetric episodes 和 non-death-asymmetric episodes 两组的加权贡献——这是一个 **exact partitioning**（对 episodes 的 mutually exclusive 分组求和），不存在交互项。\n\n但 R1 的关切可能指向另一个层面：当论文将 death episodes 的贡献近似为 δP（penalty differential）时，忽略了 death episodes 中死亡前的 partial scores。如果一个 episode 在 step 15 死亡且之前积累了一些 partial score，该 episode 的 score gap ≠ P，而是 (partial_score_surviving - (partial_score_dying + P))。在这个意义上，δP 确实是对 death episode 贡献的近似。\n\n但论文 Appendix K (p17) 的 penalty sensitivity 分析表明，当 P 足够大时（P≥100），partial score 的贡献相对于 |P| 可忽略（ScienceWorld scores 通常 0-100 scale 上在个位数到几十）。所以近似误差在 P=100 时很小，但在 P=10 时可能不可忽略。\n\n**结论**：R1 的技术关切有道理——论文应在 §2.3 或 Appendix 中明确说明分解是 exact（对 episodes）还是 approximate（对 penalty contribution），并在 P 较小时量化 residual。但这不改变主要结论的方向性。\n\n---\n\n### 小问题\n\n对 R1 的 5 个小问题无异议：\n1. 页数边界需核查（EMNLP 2026 正文 ≤8 页）\n2. §3.1 的 150 vs 95 表述歧义应修正\n3. webb-2023 年份不匹配我的引用验证也发现了（bib=2023 vs OpenAlex=2014）\n4. Figure 2 y 轴标注确实需要改进\n5. Algorithm 1 的隐含假设（P < max_possible_partial_score）确实应说明\n\n---\n\n### 总结\n\nR1 和 R2 的审稿意见高度互补。R1 从 **技术层面**（penalty sensitivity, 统计推断, 分解近似）精确地诊断了问题，R2 从 **定位层面**（framing overclaiming, 新颖性不足, 环境特异性）提出了更宏观的关切。两份 review 的核心交集在于：\n\n1. **DAF/death dominance 的 generality 不足**——R1 从 penalty-sensitivity 角度，R2 从 cross-environment 角度，殊途同归\n2. **模型规模覆盖不足**——R1 和 R2 均指出 7-9B tier 的局限\n3. **Router negative result 的解释不完整**——R1 和 R2 均认为替代假设未排除\n\n综合两份 review，我维持 Overall 3.0 (Findings) 的评分。R1 给出 3.5 (Borderline Conference) 比我高 0.5 分，我认为差异主要在于对新颖性的评估——R1 更看重框架的完整性和统计严谨性，而我更关注 finding 的 trivially-obvious 程度和 framing 的公允性。",

[Round 1 审稿请求]:
你是 Reviewer 2，负责审查**新颖性、完整性和写作质量**。

## 论文信息
- 标题：Death Avoidance as the Dominant Routing Signal: A Counterfactual Analysis on ScienceWorld
- LaTeX 源码：projects/simgate-modular-routing/docs/paper/main.tex
- PDF：/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_12/projects/simgate-modular-routing/artifacts/review_round_27/submitted.pdf
- 投稿类别：Interpretability and Analysis

## Venue 要求（EMNLP 2026，经 web 搜索确认）
- 通过 ARR May 2026 cycle 提交，deadline 2026-05-25
- Long paper 正文限 8 页，参考文献不限，Limitations 和 Ethics 不计入页数
- ACL 模板，double-blind 审稿
- Overall Assessment 评分：5=Award, 4.5=Borderline Award, 4=Conference, 3.5=Borderline Conference, 3=Findings, 2.5=Borderline Findings, 2=Resubmit next cycle, 1.5=Resubmit after next cycle, 1=Do not resubmit

## 你的审查侧重
请重点审查以下方面：

1. **Contribution 新颖性**："先诊断再建 router" 的 framing 在现有文献中是否真正新颖？counterfactual diagnostic framework 相比已有的 tool-use/routing 分析方法有何本质区别？
2. **Related work 覆盖**：是否遗漏了重要的相关工作？特别是：(a) agent routing/tool selection 领域, (b) counterfactual reasoning in RL/NLP, (c) catastrophic failure analysis, (d) modular computation/routing 领域近期工作
3. **Claims 与 Evidence 匹配**：论文声称框架具有跨环境/跨模型通用性——证据是否支撑这一声明？"diagnostic rather than prescriptive" 的定位是否准确？
4. **写作质量**：论文结构是否清晰？核心 message 是否容易理解？术语使用是否一致？是否有冗余或不必要的复杂表述？
5. **Generalization claims**：只用 ScienceWorld (11/30 task types) + 两个 boundary probes (MATH, ALFWorld)，generalization 声明是否 overclaimed？
6. **格式合规**：正文是否在 8 页限制内？匿名规则是否遵守？Limitations 和 Ethics 是否完整？Appendix 是否过度？
7. **叙事连贯性**：从 Introduction 的 motivation 到 Conclusion 的 takeaway，论文的 story arc 是否连贯？读者是否容易跟上论证逻辑？

请输出完整的结构化审稿意见：Summary → Strengths → Weaknesses → Questions → Minor Issues → 评分建议（按 EMNLP 2026 标准）。对 Weaknesses 要严格、具体、有证据支持。

39	\paragraph{Environmental scope.}
40	Our primary analysis uses a single environment (ScienceWorld, 11 of 30 task types) with one backbone (Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct), though extended validation of representative excluded tasks confirms the pattern (Appendix~\ref{app:extended-validation}). The $-100$ death penalty amplifies DAF; environments with softer penalties would exhibit lower DAF values (Appendix~\ref{app:penalty}). The 19 excluded task types (63\%) lack routing signal because both strategies typically time out within 30 steps; increasing the step budget could surface non-death-dominated signal that our current setup does not capture. Cross-environment analyses on MATH and ALFWorld (\S\ref{sec:cross-env}, \S\ref{sec:discussion}) provide preliminary supporting evidence but do not conclusively establish generalization. _source line 1467 · 2026-05-22T04:41:00.248974+00:00_

# ACL Rolling Review Form Dimensions

## 1. **Reviewer Confidence**
- Scale: 1-5
- 5: "Positive that my evaluation is correct"
- 4: Quite sure, unlikely to miss important details
- 3: Pretty sure, but may have missed some specifics
- 2: Willing to defend evaluation, but likely missed details
- 1: Outside expertise area or paper very difficult to understand

## 2. **Soundness**
- Scale: 1-5 (with .5 increments)
- 5: Exceptional thoroughness for paper type
- 4: Sufficient support for claims; minor experiments optional
- 3: Main claims adequately supported; minor details needed
- 2: Major claims lack support; significant technical problems
- 1: Insufficient rigor or not relevant to ACL

## 3. **Excitement**
- Scale: 1-5 (with .5 increments)
- 5: Highly exciting; would recommend to others
- 4: Exciting; would mention to colleagues
- 3: Interesting; might mention or attend presentation
- 2: Potentially interesting to some community members
- 1: "does not resonate...and I don't think it would with others"

## 4. **Overall Assessment**
- Scale: 1-5 (with .5 increments)
- 5: Consider for award (top 2.5%)
- 4: Conference acceptance
- 3: Findings track acceptance
- 2: Needs substantial revisions
- 1: Do not resubmit or irrelevant to field

## 5. **Reproducibility**
- Scale: 1-5
- 5: Results easily reproducible
- 4: Mostly reproducible with minor variations
- 3: Reproducible with difficulty
- 2: Hard to reproduce; data unavailable
- 1: Cannot reproduce

## 6. **Datasets**
- Scale: 1-5
- 5: Enabling; affects research direction
- 4: Useful for ongoing work
- 3: Potentially useful
- 2: Documentary value only
- 1: No usable datasets

## 7. **Software**
- Scale: 1-5
- 5: Enabling impact on research projects
- 4: Useful for researchers/developers
- 3: Potentially useful
- 2: Documentary utility only
- 1: No usable software released

## 8. **Author Identity Knowledge**
- Scale: 1-5
- 5: Author identity known via double-blind violations
- 4: Known from preprint/workshop
- 3: Identifia
…[truncated 98 chars]

_source line 1484 · 2026-05-22T04:41:18.431665+00:00_

1	\begin{abstract}
2	LLM agent routing (deciding when to rely on internal reasoning versus external tool use) is typically addressed by training routers directly, without first understanding what mechanistic signal drives the routing preference. This absence of diagnostic methods risks building routers atop confounded or trivial signal. We propose an episode-level counterfactual framework that compares pure-strategy outcomes (internal-only vs.\ deterministic-only) to decompose routing signal into its mechanistic components before any router is constructed. Applying this framework to ScienceWorld (11 task types, 245 episodes), we find that 78.9\% of non-tie routing labels arise from death asymmetry: the internal strategy triggers catastrophic failure while the deterministic strategy survives. This label fraction is penalty-independent. A derived score-weighted measure, the Death Avoidance Factor, reaches 88--90\% across three 7--9B model families (Qwen2.5-7B, Llama-3.1-8B, Gemma-2-9B), confirming convergence on a single dominant mechanism. Cross-environment probes on MATH and ALFWorld diagnose qualitatively different routing mechanisms (no extractable signal and information asymmetry, respectively), illustrating the framework's applicability. A ${\sim}$50-episode diagnostic pilot provides preliminary signal for identifying the dominant routing mechanism given a large effect size ($d = 3.02$); environments with smaller effects may require larger samples, enabling informed build-vs-buy decisions before expensive router training.
3	\end{abstract}
4

1	\section{Introduction}
2	
3	Large language models deployed as interactive agents must repeatedly decide \emph{how} to compute: reason internally, or delegate to an external tool.
4	In ScienceWorld \citep{scienceworld-2022}, a text-based science simulation, internal reasoning is error-prone, as the model may hallucinate state changes (e.g., that water has boiled when the stove was never activated), while deterministic external computation eliminates such errors via exact state transitions. The practical question: \emph{when should an agent route computation externally versus internally?}
5	
6	Existing routing approaches frame this as a capability or efficiency decision: Toolformer \citep{toolformer-2023} inserts API calls by perplexity reduction; ART \citep{art-2023} decomposes tasks into tool-augmented programs; Chameleon \citep{chameleon-2023} selects heterogeneous modules per subtask; adaptive-computation methods route to different reasoning depths \citep{cogrouter-2026,ares-2026}, model tiers \citep{mtrouter-2026}, or episode-level model selection under sparse rewards \citep{budget-aware-routing-2026}; concurrent analyses further presuppose diverse routing signals~\citep{when2tool-2026,tool-use-tax-2026,to-call-or-not-2026}. These approaches, which operate in non-catastrophic settings, share the assumption that routing signal stems from diverse sources and a learned router can exploit it---an assumption that remains untested: no prior work has decomposed what drives routing decisions in interactive environments.
7	
8	We propose a \emph{diagnostic framework} comprising (i)~episode-level counterfactual labeling under pure strategies (\S\ref{sec:collection}--\ref{sec:labeling}), and (ii)~the Death Avoidance Factor (DAF), which decomposes the routing score gap into death-avoidance and non-death components (\S\ref{sec:daf}). Applied to ScienceWorld, \textbf{78.9\% of non-tie routing labels arise from death asymmetry} (75 of 95), a penalty-independent finding. Under

2	\label{sec:method}
3	
4	\begin{figure}[t]
5	\centering
6	\includegraphics[width=\columnwidth]{figures/pipeline_overview.pdf}
7	\caption{Counterfactual episode collection and analysis pipeline. Each task instance is run under both pure strategies independently, producing episode-level labels via Algorithm~\ref{alg:labeling}.}
8	\label{fig:pipeline}
9	\end{figure}
10	
11	Our diagnostic framework quantifies what drives routing signal in interactive environments through three components: (i)~episode-level counterfactual labeling that generates routing preferences from paired pure-strategy outcomes (\S\ref{sec:collection}--\ref{sec:labeling}); (ii)~the Death Avoidance Factor (DAF), which decomposes the routing score gap into death-avoidance and non-death components (\S\ref{sec:daf}); and (iii)~the Signal Concentration Index (SCI), which measures whether routing signal is concentrated in a few task types or distributed systematically (Appendix~\ref{app:sci}). Episode-level bootstrap resampling provides 95\% confidence intervals for all metrics.
12	
13	\subsection{Problem Setup}
14	\label{sec:problem-setup}
15	
16	Consider an interactive environment with observation space $\mathcal{O}$ and action space $\mathcal{A}$. An agent policy $\pi: \mathcal{O}^* \rightarrow \mathcal{A}$ maps observation histories to actions. We define two computation modes $\mathcal{M} = \{{\tt int}, {\tt det}\}$: \textbf{internal} (${\tt int}$), where the agent predicts intermediate observations via chain-of-thought reasoning (ReAct~\citep{react-2023}), with prediction errors compounding across steps; and \textbf{deterministic}\footnote{The term ``deterministic'' denotes different information advantages across environments: in ScienceWorld, an oracle simulator providing ground-truth action outcomes; in MATH, a code interpreter verifying computations; in ALFWorld, admissible-action enumeration eliminating hallucination risk. 1	\section{Experiments}
2	\label{sec:experiments}
3	
4	We analyze 11 of ScienceWorld's 30 task types (245 episodes); the 19 excluded types (63\%) lack actionable routing signal due to development contamination (5), taxonomic redundancy (7), or agent-floor performance (7). This scope limits generalizability: conclusions apply to the included subset, not to all ScienceWorld tasks. An anti-cherry-picking analysis confirms that death dominance holds in 18 of these 19 types, with a mean $\Delta\text{DR} = +38.2$\,pp exceeding the included tasks' $+28.7$\,pp (Appendix~\ref{app:per-task}). Extended validation at $n{=}20$ on five representative excluded tasks confirms this direction ($\overline{\Delta\text{DR}} = +32.0$\,pp; Appendix~\ref{app:extended-validation}). A reasoning-depth ablation ($n{=}120$) found no significant difference between internal strategy variants ($p > 0.26$; Appendix~\ref{app:ablations}).
5	
6	\subsection{Death Avoidance Decomposition}

Since $\Delta_{\text{full}} \approx \delta P + \Delta_{\text{nd}}$ with $P$ constant, $r \to 1$ as DAF $\to 1$; the near-perfect correlation is a structural consequence of death dominance, not an independent finding (\S\ref{sec:death-decomp}).}
13	\label{fig:decomposition}
14	\end{figure}

1	\section{Related Work}
2	
3	\paragraph{Tool Use and External Computation in LLMs.}
4	A growing body of work augments language models with external tools \citep{tool-learning-survey-2024}, spanning several functional categories.
5	Self-supervised methods learn tool invocation from loss signals \citep{toolformer-2023,toolace-2025}, token-level methods embed tools in the vocabulary \citep{toolkengpt-2023}, task-level decomposition routes subtasks to specialized modules \citep{art-2023,chameleon-2023}, and RL-based methods learn routing from reward \citep{retool-2025}.
6	All focus on \emph{learning to use tools}; we ask a prior question: \emph{what signal drives the routing decision?}
7	Concurrent work examines \emph{when} to invoke tools: tool-call decisions correlate with uncertainty~\citep{tool-overuse-illusion-2026}, are encoded in hidden states~\citep{when2tool-2026}, impose a ``tool-use tax''~\citep{tool-use-tax-2026}, or follow necessity-utility-affordability logic~\citep{to-call-or-not-2026}. Our decomposition yields a concrete answer: 78.9\% of non-tie routing labels trace to catastrophic-failure prevention.
8	
9	\paragraph{Adaptive Computation and Routing.}
10	Adaptive routing traces back to learned halting policies \citep{graves2016act}. In the LLM era, CogRouter \citep{cogrouter-2026} adapts reasoning depth per step, ARES \citep{ares-2026} selects among reasoning effort levels, Mixture-of-Recursions \citep{mixture-of-recursions-2025} learns token-level recursion depths, and MTRouter \citep{mtrouter-2026} addresses multi-turn routing.
11	RouteLLM \citep{routellm-2024} learns routers from preference data to select between strong and weak models.
12	\citet{budget-aware-routing-2026} train episode-level routing policies under sparse terminal rewards, the closest setting to ours; they optimize \emph{which} model to invoke, whereas we diagnose \emph{what} drives the routing decision.
13	
14	\paragraph{Counterfactual Evaluation in RL.}
15	Prior work generates cou

1	\section{Discussion}
2	\label{sec:discussion}
3	
4	\paragraph{Why the router fails: task-type-level signal.}
5	The router diagnostic (\S\ref{sec:router-diagnostic}) reveals a structural mismatch: death avoidance is a task-type-level binary property (10/11 types carry death risk), but step-level features correlate weakly because death risk depends on multi-step trajectories. The 4B router improves at episode level ($59.0\%$) without step-level gains ($56$--$59\%$), confirming task-type classification; cross-task-type transfer yields chance accuracy (49.2\%; Appendix~\ref{app:ablations}).
6	
7	\paragraph{Non-triviality of death dominance.}
8	While death dominance follows from the penalty structure (\S\ref{sec:daf}), two consequences were not predictable a priori: $10{\times}$ scaling to 72B fails to eliminate it (DAF${}= 92.4\%$), and Reflexion self-correction yields DAF${}>100\%$ (death avoidance \emph{more} than accounts for the full gap). All four interventions leave ${\geq}12$~pp residual gaps (Appendix~\ref{sec:threshold-routing}); cross-model DR stability ($36$--$43\%$) confirms death vulnerability is a task-environment property. All 105 deaths trace to systematic misuse of the answer-submission action (Appendices~\ref{app:failure-modes}--\ref{app:grounding-ablation}). The 7 excluded agent-floor tasks---where both strategies achieve near-zero scores---represent a symmetric failure regime in which death asymmetry is absent. Our framework correctly diagnoses these as ``no signal'' episodes, a design expectation rather than a counterexample to death dominance.
9	
10	\paragraph{Practical implications.}
11	DAF ranges from $7.4\%$ ($P{=}0$) to $90.1\%$ ($P{=}100$; Appendix~\ref{app:penalty}); the penalty-invariant label fraction (78.9\%) is the primary diagnostic. Death dominance diagnosis is actionable: a ${\sim}$50-episode pilot classifies the environment before committing to router development (Appendix~\ref{app:decision-framework}). When $f_{\text{death}} > 75\%
…[truncated 324 chars]

1	\section{Conclusion}
2	
3	In ScienceWorld, death avoidance alone accounts for 78.9\% of non-tie routing labels, stable across three model families (DAF${=}88$--$90\%$); our episode-level counterfactual framework diagnoses this before any router is built. Cross-environment probes illustrate applicability; a ${\sim}$50-episode pilot provides preliminary build-vs-buy signal given large effect sizes ($d = 3.02$). Future work should validate death dominance in additional catastrophic-failure environments and develop routing methods that exploit task-type-level signals.
4

# ARR Reviewer Guidelines: Evaluation Dimensions, Quality Standards, and Scoring

## Evaluation Dimensions

Reviewers assess papers across three primary scoring dimensions:

1. **Soundness**: Technical correctness and methodological rigor. Per guidelines, "soundness scores must be justified by the text of the review."

2. **Excitement**: Personal interest and subjective appeal. This reflects individual taste separate from technical merit assessment.

3. **Overall Recommendation**: Composite judgment combining soundness, excitement, novelty, and potential impact for venue placement.

## What Constitutes a Good Review

Strong reviews demonstrate these characteristics:

**Specificity**: Reviews must provide concrete details rather than vague critiques. Instead of "missing relevant references," state "the paper omits citations to studies X, Y, and Z."

**Technical Depth**: Reviewers should identify precisely what methodology concerns exist and explain why, rather than dismissing approaches as "too simple."

**Fairness**: Reviews must account for different paper types (models, resources, analyses) and not impose a single methodological standard across diverse contributions.

**Professional Tone**: "A good review should be professional and neutral, if not kind," acknowledging that reviewers hold power in anonymous contexts.

**Justified Scores**: Low scores require explicit evidence in review text. Unexplained low soundness ratings indicate potential bias rather than legitimate concerns.

## Common Review Pitfalls to Avoid

The guidelines flag problematic patterns:

- Dismissing findings as "not surprising" or contradicting expectations (confirmation bias)
- Requiring SOTA results when not claimed
- Penalizing negative results, simplicity, or niche topics
- Demanding experiments beyond what supports stated claims
- Rejecting work for lacking preferred methodologies
